statistics cards\r\n  * quick links\r\n  * hiking / mountain / Croatia map feel\r\n* Prefer card-based layout for:\r\n\r\n  * kontrolne to-ï¿½ke\r\n  * rute\r\n  * podru-ï¿½ja\r\n  * planinarski objekti\r\n  * medalje\r\n* Use table layout where it makes more sense, such as:\r\n\r\n  * korisnici\r\n  * posjeti\r\n  * fotografije\r\n  * korisnik-medalja\r\n  * possibly udruge\r\n\r\n## for each entity\r\n* Implement complete navigation between pages\r\n* Keep logic out of views as much as possible\r\n* Views should stay clean and presentation-focused\r\n* Do not introduce unnecessary complexity in Razor views\r\n* If a page needs aggregated or combined display data, prefer preparing it in controller/viewmodel structure\r\n\r\n## Output expectations\r\n\r\nWhen helping Make sure navigation remains complete and logical\r\n\r\n## Important design direction\r\n\r\nThis application should feel like:\r\n\r\n**\"moja osobna digitalna planinarska evidencija i karta napretka\"**\r\n\r\nThat feeling must be visible in:\r\n\r\n* the home page\r\n* cards\r\n* details pages\r\n* badges\r\n* progress presentation\r\n* page hierarchy\r\n* navigation\r\n\r\n## What to avoid\r\n\r\nAvoid:\r\n\r\n* default Bootstrap feel\r\n* plain CRUD-looking pages\r\n* overly dense text blocks\r\n* old-fashioned portal clutter\r\n* too many unrelated colors\r\n* overly decorative UI that harms readability\r\n\r\n## First task\r\n\r\nStart by:\r\n\r\n1. briefly summarizing the design direction from the two files\r\n2. proposing the UI structure for the main layout and home page\r\n3. proposing a reusable component style system for:\r\n\r\n   * cards\r\n   * badges\r\n   * stat cards\r\n   * details sections\r\n   * navigation\r\n4. then suggest the best order of implementing the views in the MVC project\r\n\r\nDo not ignore the two referenced markdown files.
